\displaystyle=\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\log\left(S_{i}^{n}(u)\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\right)-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)+O_{p}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}})

\displaystyle(\text{The equation holds almost surely, as given by Lemma 4 in \cite[cite]{\@@bibref{Authors Phrase1YearPhrase2}{LLL2018}{\@@citephrase{(}}{\@@citephrase{)}}}}.)

\displaystyle=\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\log\left(S_{i}^{n}(u)\right)-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)+O_{p}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}})

\displaystyle=\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\log{(1+\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))}+O_{p}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}),

with
\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)=\frac{S_{i}^{n}(u)-f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}{f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}
.
Since
|\log(1+x)-x|\leq Cx^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)-\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)\right|\leq\frac{C}{u^{2}}|\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)|^{2}.

(73)

We decompose

\displaystyle\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)=\sum_{w=1}^{3}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(w)}(u),

(74)

with
\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(1)}(u)=\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\frac{A_{i}^{n}(u)}{f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}
,
\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2)}(u)=\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\frac{B_{i}^{n}(u)}{f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}
, and
\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3)}(u)=\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\frac{C_{i}^{n}(u)}{f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}
. Then, to prove the results (
14
), (
15
) and (
16
), it is sufficient to show

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{k_{n}}\wedge\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(1)}(u)\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(75)

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{k_{n}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2)}(u),\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3)}(u)\right)\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}\left(V_{t},V^{\prime}_{t}\right),

(76)

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{k_{n}}\wedge\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)\frac{u^{2}}{2}\left|\sum_{w=1}^{3}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(w)}(u)\right|^{2}\leq\frac{Cu^{2}(k_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\wedge 1)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{w=1}^{3}\left|\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(w)}(u)\right|^{2}\longrightarrow^{p}0.

(77)

For (
75
), by following the proof of Lemma 2, Lemma 5 in
Liu et al. 2018
, with the boundedness of
\sigma,\gamma,\delta
and condition (
9
), we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(1)}(u)|]\leq\begin{cases}&C\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{u},\ \text{for}\ 0<\max\{\beta,r\}<1,\\
&C\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{u}+C\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{5}{4p_{1}}-\frac{1}{4}}}{u}+C\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1}{p_{2}}-\frac{1}{2}}}{u},\ \text{for}\ 1\leq\beta<p_{1}<2,1\leq r<p_{2}<2.\end{cases}

(78)

If
\beta<2
and
r<4/3
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left(\sqrt{k_{n}}\wedge\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(1)}(u)\right|\right]\leq C\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\Delta_{n}^{\frac{5}{4(\beta+\epsilon)}-\frac{1}{2}}+\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1}{(r+\epsilon)}-\frac{3}{4}}}{u}\right)\rightarrow 0.

For (
76
),
according to Lemma S2.3 in
Liu and Liu 2024
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)\cdot\left(f^{(2)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)+o_{p}(u^{4}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}})\right)

\displaystyle=f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)\cdot f^{(2)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)+o_{p}(u^{4}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}).

We write

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3)}(u)\\
&=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{-2\left(f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)\cdot f^{(2)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)-f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)f^{(2)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)\right)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}u^{2}f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)f^{(2)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}+o_{p}\left(\frac{u^{2}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\right)\\
&=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{-2\left(f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)-f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)\right)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}u^{2}f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{-2f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)\left(f^{(2)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)-f^{(2)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)\right)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}u^{2}f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)f^{(2)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}+o_{p}\left(\frac{u^{2}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\right).\end{split}

(79)

For
j\in I^{n}_{i+}
, we have

\displaystyle f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)-f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)

(By Taylor’s expansion, the boundedness of
\sigma^{2}
and condition (
9
).)

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}/2\right)}\frac{-u^{2}}{2}(\sigma^{2}_{t_{j-1}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})+O_{p}(u^{4}k_{n}\Delta_{n})

\displaystyle(\text{By It$\hat{\text{o}}$'s formula.})

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}/2\right)}\frac{-u^{2}}{2}\int_{t_{j-1}^{n}}^{t_{j}^{n}}\int_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{t_{j-1}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}_{u}dB_{u}+2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{u}dB^{\prime}_{u}\right)ds

\displaystyle\quad+\exp{\left(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}/2\right)}\frac{-u^{2}}{2}\int_{t_{j-1}^{n}}^{t_{j}^{n}}\int_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{t_{j-1}^{n}}(2\sigma_{u}\tilde{b}_{u}+(\tilde{\sigma}_{u})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{u})^{2})duds+O_{p}(u^{4}k_{n}\Delta_{n})

\displaystyle(\text{Since $\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}$ are bounded.})

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}/2\right)}\frac{-u^{2}}{2}\int_{t_{j-1}^{n}}^{t_{j}^{n}}\int_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{t_{j-1}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}_{u}dB_{u}+2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{u}dB^{\prime}_{u}\right)ds+O_{p}((u^{2}+u^{4})k_{n}\Delta_{n})

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}/2\right)}\frac{-u^{2}}{2}\left(\int_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{t_{j-1}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}_{u}\right)dB_{u}+\int_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{t_{j-1}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{u}\right)dB^{\prime}_{u}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\exp{\left(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}/2\right)}\frac{-u^{2}}{2\Delta_{n}}\left(\int_{t_{j-1}^{n}}^{t_{j}^{n}}\int_{u}^{t_{j}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}_{u}\right)dsdB_{u}+\int_{t_{j-1}^{n}}^{t_{j}^{n}}\int_{u}^{t_{j}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{u}\right)dsdB^{\prime}_{u}\right)

\displaystyle(\text{Since $\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}$ are bounded.})

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}/2\right)}\frac{-u^{2}}{2}\left(\int_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{t_{j-1}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}_{u}\right)dB_{u}+\int_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{t_{j-1}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{u}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{u}\right)dB^{\prime}_{u}\right)+O_{p}((u^{2}+u^{4})k_{n}\Delta_{n})

\displaystyle(\text{Using the boundedness of $\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}$ and condition \eqref{cond-vov}.})

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}/2\right)}\frac{-u^{2}}{2}\left(\int_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{t_{j-1}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\right)dB_{u}+\int_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{t_{j-1}^{n}}\left(2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\right)dB^{\prime}_{u}\right)+O_{p}((u^{2}+u^{4})k_{n}\Delta_{n}).

Since
\gamma
is bounded, with condition (
9
), we have

\displaystyle f^{(2)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)-f^{(2)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)=O_{p}(u^{\beta}\Delta_{n}^{1-\beta/2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{1/2}).

Substituting the above results into (
79
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3)}(u)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)+O_{p}(\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}})+O_{p}\left(\frac{u^{\beta}\Delta_{n}^{1-\beta/2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)+o_{p}\left(\frac{u^{2}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\right),

where

\displaystyle\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)=\frac{(i+k_{n}-j)\left(2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}.

And we write

\displaystyle\sqrt{k_{n}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2)}(u)=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u),

with

\displaystyle\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)=\frac{-2\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right]\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}}u^{2}f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}.

Then, to prove (
76
), it remains to show

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u),\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}\left(V_{t},V^{\prime}_{t}\right).

Notice that
\{\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u),\mathcal{F}_{t_{j}^{n}}\}
and
\{\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u),\mathcal{F}_{t_{j}^{n}}\}
are martingale difference arrays, according to Theorem 2.2.15 in
Jacod and Protter 2012
, we only need to show

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}\frac{2(\exp{(-2u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t})}-2\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t})}+1)}{u^{4}\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t})}},\\
&\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}\frac{4(\sigma_{t})^{2}((\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2})}{3},\\
&\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\cdot\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,\end{split}

(80)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{2}+\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\leq 2\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{4}+\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{4}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(81)

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\cdot\left(W_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-W_{t_{i}^{n}}+B_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B_{t_{i}^{n}}+B^{\prime}_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B^{\prime}_{t_{i}^{n}}\right)\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(82)

where
W
is a bounded martingale orthogonal to both
B
and
B^{\prime}
.

For (
80
), since
\gamma,\sigma
are bounded, with condition (
9
), applying Taylor’s expansion yields

\displaystyle f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)=f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)+O_{p}(u^{2}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}),\qquad f^{(2)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)=1+O_{p}(|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}).

(83)

Furthermore, we can get

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{4}{k_{n}u^{4}(f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right)^{2}\right]-\left(\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right]\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{4}{k_{n}u^{4}(f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\frac{\cos\left(\frac{2u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)+1}{2}\right]-\left(f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)\cdot f^{(2)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{4}{k_{n}u^{4}(f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(2u)\cdot f^{(2)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(2u)-\left(f^{(1)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)\cdot f^{(2)}_{t_{j-1}^{n},n}(u)\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{4}{k_{n}u^{4}(f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(2u)-\left(f^{(1)}_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)\right)^{2}+O_{p}\left(u^{2}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{p}\frac{2(\exp{(-2u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t})}-2\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t})}+1)}{u^{4}\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t})}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\right]=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\frac{(i+k_{n}+1-j)\left(2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{p}\frac{4(\sigma_{t})^{2}((\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2})}{3},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\cdot\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{-4((i+k_{n}-j))\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}}{k_{n}^{2}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}u^{2}f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{-4((i+k_{n}-j))\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}}{k_{n}^{2}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}u^{2}f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle(\text{Since $\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime},\gamma$ are bounded.})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}u^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cos\left(\frac{u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{C}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}u^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\sin\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}u^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cos\left(\frac{u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{C}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}u^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\sin\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle(\text{Since $B,B^{\prime},L$ are mutually independent, their distributions are symmetric around 0,}

and the functions of
\sin(x)
and
x\cos{x}
are odd.)

\displaystyle\equiv 0.

For (
81
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{4}+\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{16}{k_{n}^{2}u^{8}(f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))^{4}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right]\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{(i+k_{n}-j)^{4}}{k_{n}^{6}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\frac{2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle(\text{Since $\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime},\gamma$ and cosine function are bounded. })

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{k_{n}}\rightarrow 0.

The result (
82
) follows from

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)\cdot\left(W_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-W_{t_{i}^{n}}+B_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B_{t_{i}^{n}}+B^{\prime}_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B^{\prime}_{t_{i}^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{-2}{\sqrt{k_{n}}u^{2}f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}\left(\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cos\left(\frac{u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{2}{\sqrt{k_{n}}u^{2}f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}\left(\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\sin\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle(\text{Since $B,B^{\prime},L$ are mutually independent, their distributions are symmetric around 0,}

and the functions of
\sin(x)
and
x\cos{x}
are odd.)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{-2}{\sqrt{k_{n}}u^{2}f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}\left(\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cos\left(\frac{u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\Delta_{j}^{n}W\right]\right)

\displaystyle(\text{By Holder's inequality and the boundedness of $\sigma,\gamma$.})

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}}}{u^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\cdot\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\Delta_{j}^{n}W\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\longrightarrow^{p}0,

and

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\cdot\left(W_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-W_{t_{i}^{n}}+B_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B_{t_{i}^{n}}+B^{\prime}_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B^{\prime}_{t_{i}^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{(i+k_{n}+1-j)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{(i+k_{n}+1-j)\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\right)\leq C\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\rightarrow 0.

This ends the proof of (
76
).

For (
77
), since
\sigma,\gamma
and cosine function are bounded, together with the proof of (
75
), we have

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{k_{n}}\wedge\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(1)}(u)\right|^{2}\right]\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left(\sqrt{k_{n}}\wedge\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(1)}(u)\right|\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,

and

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{k_{n}}\wedge\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2)}(u)\right|^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3)}(u)\right|^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\wedge 1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sqrt{k_{n}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2)}(u)\right|^{2}\right]+(k_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\wedge 1)\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left|\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3)}(u)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle(\text{From the proof of \eqref{xi-23} and since $\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}$ are bounded.})

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}+\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\right)\rightarrow 0.

This finishes the proof of Proposition
1
.
\square

8.2
Proof for Theorem
1

For the convenience of proofs in this part, we define, for
i=k_{n}+1,...,n-k_{n}
,

\displaystyle s_{i}^{n}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right),

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)=\frac{-2\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right]\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}}u^{2}f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)},\\
&\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)=\frac{(i+k_{n}-j)\left(2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}},\\
&\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)=\frac{-2\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right]\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}}u^{2}f_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(u)},\\
&\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)=\frac{(i-1-j)\left(2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}},\end{split}

(84)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)=\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}}-b_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}(u)\right)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i\pm}}\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)-\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i\pm}}\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right).\end{split}

(85)

Observe that
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}=\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)+}^{n}}
,
from the proof of Proposition
1
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(\sqrt{k_{n}}\wedge\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}}-b_{t_{\pm+}^{n},n}(u)\right)\\
&=\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\wedge 1\right)\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i\pm}}\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+(k_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\wedge 1)\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i\pm}}\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)+O_{p}\left(h_{n}\right),\end{split}

(86)

with

\displaystyle h_{n}=\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1}{4}\wedge\left(\frac{5}{4(\beta+\epsilon)}-\frac{1}{2}\right)\wedge\left(\frac{1}{(r+\epsilon)}-\frac{3}{4}\right)}}{u}+\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}+\frac{u^{\beta}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right).

And by following the proof of Lemma 2 and Lemma 5 in
Liu et al. 2018
, applying Taylor’s expansion with the boudedness of
\sigma
and cosine function, we can obtain, for
i,j=1,...,n
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Delta_{j}^{n}X=\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\left(\frac{5}{4(\beta+\epsilon)}+\frac{1}{4}\right)\wedge\frac{1}{(r+\epsilon)}}\right),\\
&\Delta_{j}^{n}X=\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}\right)=\sigma_{t_{i}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+O_{p}\left(\sqrt{|i-j+1|}\Delta_{n}+\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}\right),\\
&\frac{\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}=\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\frac{\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{(1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)})-\frac{1}{2}}\right),\\
&\cos\left(\frac{\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)=\cos\left(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\frac{\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{(1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)})-\frac{1}{2}}\right),\\
&\frac{1}{k}\left(\cos\left(\frac{\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right)^{k}=\frac{1}{k}\left(\cos\left(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\frac{\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right)^{k}+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{(1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)})-\frac{1}{2}}\right).\end{split}

(87)

Before presenting the proof of Theorem
1
, we give the following lemmas to be used and their proofs.

Lemma 1

Under the same assumptions and conditions as in Proposition
1
,
we have, for
i=k_{n}+1,...,n-k_{n}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sqrt{k_{n}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u),\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u)\right)&\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}\left(V_{i+},V_{i-}\right),\qquad\text{if}\quad\kappa=0,\\
\sqrt{k_{n}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u),\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u)\right)&\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}\left(V_{i+}+\kappa V^{\prime}_{i+},V_{i-}+\kappa V^{\prime}_{i-}\right),\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\text{if}\quad 0<\kappa<\infty,\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u),\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u)\right)&\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}\left(V^{\prime}_{i+},V^{\prime}_{i-}\right)\qquad\text{if}\quad\kappa=\infty,\end{split}

(88)

where
(V_{i+},V^{\prime}_{i+},V_{i-},V^{\prime}_{i-})
is a vector of normal random variables defined on an extension of the probability space
(\Omega,\mathcal{F},\mathcal{F}_{0\leq t\leq T},\mathbb{P})
. Moreover, conditionally on the
\sigma-
field
\mathcal{F}
, it has zero mean,
\mathcal{F}-
conditional covariance
Cov(V_{i\pm},V^{\prime}_{i\pm}|\mathcal{F})=\mathbb{E}[V_{i\pm}V^{\prime}_{i\pm}|\mathcal{F}]=0
, and
\mathcal{F}-
conditional variance

\displaystyle\begin{split}&Var(V_{i\pm}|\mathcal{F})=\mathbb{E}[(V_{i\pm})^{2}|\mathcal{F}]=\frac{2(\exp{(-2u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})}-2\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})}+1)}{u^{4}\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})}},\\
&Var(V^{\prime}_{i\pm}|\mathcal{F})=\mathbb{E}[(V^{\prime}_{i\pm})^{2}|\mathcal{F}]=\frac{4(\sigma_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})^{2}((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})^{2})}{3}.\end{split}

(89)

Proof of Lemma
1
:

According to (
86
), the conclusion (
88
) can be equivalently proved by showing

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}}\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u),\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u),\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u),\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}\left(V_{i-},V^{\prime}_{i-},V_{i+},V^{\prime}_{i+}\right),

(90)

where we further define, for
w=2,3
,
\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(w,j)}(u)=0
if
j\in I^{n}_{i+}
, and
\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(w,j)}(u)=0
if
j\in I^{n}_{i-}
.
Noticing that
\left\{\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u),\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u),\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u),\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right),\mathcal{F}_{t_{j}^{n}}\right\}
is a martingale difference array, according to Theorem 2.2.15 in
Jacod and Protter 2012
, we only need to show

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}\frac{2(\exp{(-2u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})}-2\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})}+1)}{u^{4}\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})}},

(91)

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}\frac{4(\sigma_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})^{2}((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}})^{2})}{3},

(92)

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\cdot\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)+\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\cdot\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\cdot\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(93)

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{4}+\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{4}+\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{4}+\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{4}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(94)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\Bigg[\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)+\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\left(W_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-W_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}}+B_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}}+B^{\prime}_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B^{\prime}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}}\right)\Bigg]\longrightarrow^{p}0,\end{split}

(95)

where
W
is a bounded martingale orthogonal to both
B
and
B^{\prime}
.
For (
91
) and (
92
), from the proof of Proposition
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{p}\frac{2(\exp{(-2u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)+}^{n}})}-2\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)+}^{n}})}+1)}{u^{4}\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)+}^{n}})}}

\displaystyle(\text{Apply Taylor's expansion, use the boundedness of $\sigma$ and condition \eqref{cond-vov}.})

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\exp{(-2u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})}-2\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})}+1)}{u^{4}\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})}}+O_{p}(u^{2}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{p}\frac{4(\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)+}^{n}})^{2}((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)+}^{n}})^{2})}{3}

\displaystyle(\text{Using the boundedness of $\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}$ and condition \eqref{cond-vov}.})

\displaystyle=\frac{4(\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}})^{2}((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i-}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i-}^{n}})^{2})}{3}+O_{p}(\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}).

The results for
\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)
and
\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)
can be found in Proposition
1
.
For (
93
), it is obvious that
\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(w_{1},j)}(u)\cdot\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(w_{2},j)}(u)\right]\equiv 0
for all
w_{1},w_{2}=\{2,3\}
and
j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}
, and the results of
\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\cdot\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0
, (
95
) and (
94
) can be obtained by following the proof of Proposition
1
.

The proof of Lemma
1
is completed.
\square

Lemma 2

Under the same assumptions and conditions as in Theorem
1
, for
i=k_{n}+1,...,n-k_{n}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\right)\right]=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}\cdot h_{n}).

(96)

For
j=k_{n}+1,...,n-k_{n}
with
i<j
, if
j-i>2k_{n}+1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\cdot\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{j}\right)\right]=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2\wedge\frac{2}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}h_{n}^{2}),

(97)

if
j-i\leq 2k_{n}+1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\cdot\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{j}\right)\right]=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2\wedge\frac{2}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}).

(98)

Proof of Lemma
2
:
For (
96
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Delta_{i+}^{n}X\cdot\left(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(1-2b)}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sqrt{\kappa T}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(2b-1)}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{i+}^{n}B\bigg|\left(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}},\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\right)\right]\cdot\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\left(\Delta_{i+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{i+}^{n}B\right)\cdot\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u)\right)\right)\cdot\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right]\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\left(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(1-2b)}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sqrt{\kappa T}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(2b-1)}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\cdot\Bigg|\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X,\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}},\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\Bigg]\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle(\text{According to \eqref{spot-main} and \eqref{X-main}.})

\displaystyle=0+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}\cdot h_{n}\right).

If
j-i>2k_{n}+1
, since there is no overlapping terms between
s^{n}_{i}
and
s^{n}_{j}
, by successive conditioning, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\cdot\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{j}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{j}\Big|s_{i}^{n}\right]\right]

(By following the proof of (
96
).)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left(\Delta_{j+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j+}^{n}}\Delta_{j+}^{n}B\right)\cdot\xi_{t_{j+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{j-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j-}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\right)\Big|s_{j}^{n}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\cdot\left(\left(\Delta_{j+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j+}^{n}}\Delta_{j+}^{n}B\right)\cdot\xi_{t_{j+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{j-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j-}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Big[\mathbb{E}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\Big|\left(\Delta_{j+}^{n}X,\sigma_{t_{j+}^{n}},\Delta_{j+}^{n}B,\xi_{t_{j+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u),\xi_{t_{j-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u),\Delta_{j}^{n}X,\sigma_{t_{j-}^{n}},\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\qquad\qquad\cdot\left(\left(\Delta_{j+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j+}^{n}}\Delta_{j+}^{n}B\right)\cdot\xi_{t_{j+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{j-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j-}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\right)\Big]

(By following the proof of (
96
).)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Big[\mathbb{E}\Big[\left(\left(\Delta_{i+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{i+}^{n}B\right)\cdot\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\cdot\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\ \ \Big|\left(\Delta_{j+}^{n}X,\sigma_{t_{j+}^{n}},\Delta_{j+}^{n}B,\xi_{t_{j+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u),\xi_{t_{j-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u),\Delta_{j}^{n}X,\sigma_{t_{j-}^{n}},\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\cdot\left(\left(\Delta_{j+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j+}^{n}}\Delta_{j+}^{n}B\right)\cdot\xi_{t_{j+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{j-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j-}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\right)\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Big[\left(\left(\Delta_{i+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{i+}^{n}B\right)\cdot\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\cdot\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\cdot\left(\left(\Delta_{j+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j+}^{n}}\Delta_{j+}^{n}B\right)\cdot\xi_{t_{j+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{j-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{j-}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B\right)\right)\Big]

\displaystyle(\text{According to \eqref{spot-main} and \eqref{X-main}.})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2\wedge\frac{2}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}h_{n}^{2}).

As for
j-i\leq 2k_{n}+1
, we consider the special case of
j=i+2
for the convenience of presentation and understanding. Notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\cdot\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i+2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Big[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)+}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)+}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)+}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)+}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)+}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right]

\displaystyle:=A_{i}^{n}+B_{i}^{n}+C_{i}^{n}+D_{i}^{n}.

For
A_{i}^{n}
, we define

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n},-(i+2)}

\displaystyle=\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\log\left(\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+},j\neq(i+2)}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},-i}

\displaystyle=\frac{-2}{u^{2}}\log\left(\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-},j\neq i}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)\right),

and we decompose

\displaystyle A_{i}^{n}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n},-(i+2)}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},-i}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n},-(i+2)}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},-i}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n},-(i+2)}\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},-i}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n},-(i+2)}\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},-i}\right)\right]

\displaystyle:=A_{i}^{n}(1)+A_{i}^{n}(2)+A_{i}^{n}(3)+A_{i}^{n}(4).

For
A_{i}^{n}(1)
, we have
A_{i}^{n}(1)=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2\wedge\frac{2}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}})
since

\displaystyle A_{i}^{n}(1)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Big[\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\Big|\left(\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X,\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n},-(i+2)},\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}},b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u),\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},-i},\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}},b_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},n}(u)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n},-(i+2)}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},-i}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Big]

\displaystyle(\text{By conducting successive conditioning technique similar to the one in the proof of \eqref{lem-lev-2-2}}.)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Big[\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i+2}^{n}}\Delta_{i+2}^{n}B\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n},-(i+2)}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},-i}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}+b_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Big]

(According to (
87
) and Lemma
1
.)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2\wedge\frac{2}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}).

And

\displaystyle A_{i}^{n}(4)

\displaystyle=n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n},-(i+2)}\right)\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{(i+2)-}^{n},-i}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{u^{4}}n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot\log\left(1+\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+},j\neq(i+2)}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\cdot\log\left(1+\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-},j\neq i}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle(\text{According to Taylor's expansion for the function of $\log(1+x)$.})

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{u^{4}}n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+},j\neq(i+2)}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-},j\neq i}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}\Bigg]\Bigg|\{\Delta_{j}^{n}X:j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}-\{i,i+2\}\}\Bigg]\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{u^{4}}n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+},j\neq(i+2)}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\quad\Bigg|\{\Delta_{j}^{n}X:j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}-\{i+2\}\}\Bigg]\cdot\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-},j\neq i}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\Bigg(R_{i}^{n}(1)=\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i+2}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+},j\neq(i+2)}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\ -\sigma_{t_{i+2}^{n}}\Delta_{i+2}^{n}B\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{i+2}^{n}}\Delta_{i+2}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+},j\neq(i+2)}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{u^{4}}n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sigma_{t_{i+2}^{n}}\Delta_{i+2}^{n}B\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{i+2}^{n}}\Delta_{i+2}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+},j\neq(i+2)}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}+R_{i}^{n}(1)

\displaystyle\quad\Bigg|\{\Delta_{j}^{n}X:j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}-\{i+2\}\}\Bigg]\cdot\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-},j\neq i}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}\Bigg]

\displaystyle(\text{Since $x\cos^{k}(cx)$ with positive constant $c$ and $k\in\mathbb{N}^{+}$ are odd functions.})

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{u^{4}}n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[R_{i}^{n}(1)\cdot\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-},j\neq i}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\quad\Bigg|\{\Delta_{j}^{n}X:j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}-\{i\}\}\cup R_{i}^{n}(1)\Bigg]\Bigg]

\displaystyle\Bigg(R_{i}^{n}(2)=\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{i}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-},j\neq(i)}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\ -\sigma_{t_{i}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{i}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-},j\neq i}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{u^{4}}n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[R_{i}^{n}(1)\cdot\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sigma_{t_{i}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{k}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{k_{n}}\cos{\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{i}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}}{\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-},j\neq i}\cos{\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)}\right)}\right)^{k}+R_{i}^{n}(2)

\displaystyle\quad\Bigg|\{\Delta_{j}^{n}X:j\in I^{n}_{(i+2)-}\cup I^{n}_{i+}-\{i\}\}\cup R_{i}^{n}(1)\Bigg]\Bigg]

\displaystyle(\text{Since $x\cos^{k}(cx)$ with positive constant $c$ and $k\in\mathbb{N}^{+}$ are odd functions.})

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{u^{2}}\cdot n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[R_{i}^{n}(1)\cdot R_{i}^{n}(2)\right]

\displaystyle(\text{By plugging in the results in \eqref{X-main}}.)

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{u^{2}}\cdot\frac{n^{b\wedge(1-b)}}{n^{2b}}\cdot O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2\wedge\frac{2}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}})=o_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2\wedge\frac{2}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}).

Similarly, we can obtain that
A_{i}^{n}(2),A_{i}^{n}(3)=o_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2\wedge\frac{2}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}})
, and furthermore
B_{i}^{n},C_{i}^{n},D_{i}^{n}=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2\wedge\frac{2}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}})
. We omit their detailed derivation for saving the space and they can be provided upon requests.
The proof for the general case of
j-i\leq 2k_{n}
is the same as the above.

The proof of this lemma is completed.
\square

Proof of Theorem
1
:
We prove the central limit theorem (
23
) first. We notice that

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{L}}_{[0,T]}-\mathcal{L}_{[0,T]}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}s_{i}^{n}+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)-\mathcal{L}_{[0,T]}

\displaystyle(\text{Since $\gamma$ is bounded and with condition \eqref{cond-vov}.})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}s_{i}^{n}+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\left(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)\right)-\mathcal{L}_{[0,T]}+O_{p}(u^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{\frac{2-\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}s_{i}^{n}+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\int_{t_{i-1}^{n}}^{t_{i}^{n}}\sigma_{s}dB_{s}\cdot\left(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)\right)-\mathcal{L}_{[0,T]}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\int_{t_{i-1}^{n}}^{t_{i}^{n}}\sigma_{s}dB_{s}\right)\cdot\left(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)\right)+O_{p}(u^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{\frac{2-\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle(\text{By the proof of Lemma 2 in \cite[cite]{\@@bibref{Authors Phrase1YearPhrase2}{LLL2018}{\@@citephrase{(}}{\@@citephrase{)}}}, the boundedness of $\sigma$ and the condition \eqref{cond-vov}}.)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}s_{i}^{n}+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\int_{t_{i-1}^{n}}^{t_{i}^{n}}\sigma_{s}dB_{s}\cdot\left(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)\right)-\mathcal{L}_{[0,T]}+O_{p}\left(u^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{\frac{2-\beta}{2}}+\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1}{\max\{\beta,r\}}-\frac{1}{2}}+\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\right).

When
\max\{\beta,r\}\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(u^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{\frac{2-\beta}{2}}+\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1}{\max\{\beta,r\}}-\frac{1}{2}}+\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\right)\rightarrow 0.

According to the proof of Theorem 3 (Step 3) in
Aït-Sahalia et al. 2017
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\int_{t_{i-1}^{n}}^{t_{i}^{n}}\sigma_{s}dB_{s}\cdot\left(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)\right)-\mathcal{L}_{[0,T]}\right)\longrightarrow^{p}0.

Thus, it remains to prove

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}s_{i}^{n}\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}U.

(99)

Similarly to the manipulation in
Jacod and Protter 2012
(Section 12.2.4) and
Aït-Sahalia et al. 2017
(Step 4 in the proof of Theorem 3 therein), we split the sum over
i
into big blocks of size
(\tilde{m}+2)k_{n}
with
\tilde{m}
satisfying

\displaystyle\tilde{m}\rightarrow\infty,\quad\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\rightarrow 0.

(100)

We define
I(\tilde{m},n,l)=(l-1)(\tilde{m}+2)k_{n}+1
for
l=1,...,l_{n}(\tilde{m})
with the total number of big blocks
l_{n}(\tilde{m})=\lfloor\frac{\lfloor T/\Delta_{n}\rfloor-1}{(\tilde{m}+2)k_{n}}\rfloor
. It is obvious that in the
l
-th big block, for
s_{i}^{n}
with
I(\tilde{m},n,l)+k_{n}+1\leq i\leq I(\tilde{m},n,l)+(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}
, the increments of
\Delta_{j}^{n}X
constituting
s_{i}^{n}
are within
I(\tilde{m},n,l)
, thus making
s_{i}^{n}
be
\mathcal{F}_{I(\tilde{m},n,l+1)\Delta_{n}}
-measurable. For simplifying notation, we use
(l,j)
for the time point
(I(\tilde{m},n,l)+j)\Delta_{n}
or its index
(I(\tilde{m},n,l)+j)
and
\mathbb{E}^{n}_{(l,j)}[\cdot]
for the conditional expectation
\mathbb{E}[\cdot|\mathcal{F}_{(l,j)}]
, with
l=1,...,l_{n}(\tilde{m})
and
j
being any integer number.
We decompose
\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}s_{i}^{n}=Z(\tilde{m})^{n}+\tilde{Z}(\tilde{m})^{n}+\tilde{Z}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}
with

\displaystyle\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}=\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}s^{n}_{(l,r)},\quad\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}=\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\sum_{r=-k_{n}}^{k_{n}}s^{n}_{(l,r)},

\displaystyle Z(\tilde{m})^{n}=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n},\quad\tilde{Z}(\tilde{m})^{n}=\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n},\quad\tilde{Z}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}=\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\sum_{i=(l_{n}(\tilde{m})+1,-k_{n})}^{n-k_{n}}s^{n}_{i}.

To prove (
99
), we will show that

\displaystyle\tilde{Z}(\tilde{m})^{n}+\tilde{Z}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(101)

\displaystyle Z(\tilde{m})^{n}\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}U.

(102)

For (
101
), we notice that when
\tilde{m}>2
,
\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}
is
(l+1,-2k_{n}-1)
-measurable and there is no overlapping terms among the sequence of
\{\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}:l=2,...,l_{n}(\tilde{m})\}
. According to Lemma 4.1 in
Jacod 2012
,
\tilde{Z}(\tilde{m})^{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0
can be proved by showing

\displaystyle\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,-2k_{n}-1)}^{n}[\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}]\longrightarrow^{p}0,\qquad\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,-2k_{n}-1)}^{n}[(\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}]\longrightarrow^{p}0.

(103)

For
r=-k_{n},...,k_{n}
with
l=2,...,l_{n}(\tilde{m})
, according to Lemma
2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,-2k_{n}-1)}^{n}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r)}\right]=0+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}\cdot h_{n}\right).

(104)

When
\max\{\beta,r\}\leq 1
, since
\tilde{m}\rightarrow\infty
, we have
l_{n}(\tilde{m})k_{n}\cdot\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}h_{n}\rightarrow 0
,
thus,
\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,-2k_{n}-1)}^{n}[\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}]\rightarrow^{p}0
. Observe

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,-k_{n}-1)}^{n}[(\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(l,-k_{n}-1)}^{n}\left[\sum_{r=-k_{n}}^{k_{n}}\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r)}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{(l,-k_{n}-1)}^{n}\left[2\sum_{r_{1},r_{2}=-k_{n}}^{k_{n}}\sum_{r_{1}<r_{2}}\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\cdot\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right]

\displaystyle(\text{According to \eqref{spot-main}, \eqref{X-main}, \eqref{lem1-key} and Lemma \ref {lem-lev-2}.})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(k_{n}\Delta_{n}),

as
\tilde{m}\longrightarrow\infty
, we have
\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,-k_{n}-1)}^{n}[(\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}]\rightarrow^{p}0
.
As for
\tilde{Z}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0
, similarly, by following the above proof, we can prove

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{Z}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}]=\tilde{m}k_{n}\cdot O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}h_{n}\right)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{Z}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n})^{2}]=O_{p}\left((\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{2}\right).

Since
\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\rightarrow 0
, Chebyshev’s inequality implies the convergence in probability. This finishes the proof of (
101
).

For (
102
), from the above proof of (
101
), if
\max\{\beta,r\}\leq 1
, since
k_{n}\Delta_{n}\rightarrow 0
and
k_{n}\rightarrow\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}\right]=O_{p}\left(n\cdot\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}h_{n}\right)\longrightarrow^{p}0,

and it is obvious that
\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}
is
\mathcal{F}_{(l+1,0)}
-measurable, thus
\left\{\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n},\mathcal{F}_{(l+1,0)}\right\}
behaves like a martingale difference array. According to Theorem 2.2.15 in
Jacod and Protter 2012
,it remains to show

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[(\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}Var(U|\mathcal{F}),

(105)

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[(\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{4}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(106)

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}\cdot\left(\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}B+\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}B^{\prime}+\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}W\right)\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(107)

where
\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M:=M_{(l+1,0)}-M_{(l,0)}
for
M=B,B^{\prime}
or
W
with
W
being a bounded martingale orthogonal to both
B
and
B^{\prime}
. For (
105
), we decompose

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[(\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r)}\cdot\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,j)}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r)})^{2}]+n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\cdot\sum_{r,j=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\sum_{|j-r|\leq 2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[s^{n}_{(l,r)}s^{n}_{(l,j)}]

\displaystyle\quad+n^{b\wedge(1-b)}\cdot\sum_{r,j=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\sum_{|j-r|>2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[s^{n}_{(l,r)}s^{n}_{(l,j)}]

\displaystyle:=H(\tilde{m},1)_{l}^{n}+H(\tilde{m},2)_{l}^{n}+H(\tilde{m},3)_{l}^{n}.

According to Lemma
2
, when
\max\{\beta,r\}\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}H(\tilde{m},2)_{l}^{n}+\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}H(\tilde{m},3)_{l}^{n}=O_{p}(\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n})\longrightarrow^{p}0,

and

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}H(\tilde{m},1)_{l}^{n}=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r)})^{2}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r)})^{2}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\Bigg[(\Delta_{i}^{n}X)^{2}\cdot\Bigg(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad-\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{2}\Bigg]

(According to (
87
) and the results of Lemma
1
.)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\Bigg(\sigma_{(l,0)}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[(\Delta_{i}^{n}B)^{2}\cdot\Bigg(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad-\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]+O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\tilde{m}k_{n}}\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{p}}\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle(\text{With condition \eqref{con-m} and from the proof of Lemma \ref {lem-lev-1}}.)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\Bigg(\sigma_{(l,0)}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[(\Delta_{i}^{n}B)^{2}\cdot\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(1-2b)}\left(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)-\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad-\sqrt{\kappa T}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(2b-1)}\left(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)-\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]\Bigg)+o_{p}\left(1\right)

\displaystyle(\text{Since $(\Delta_{i}^{n}B)^{2}-\Delta_{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0$ and according to Lemma \ref {lem-lev-1} and Proposition 2.5 in}

\displaystyle\ \ \text{\cite[cite]{\@@bibref{Authors Phrase1YearPhrase2}{PV2010}{\@@citephrase{(}}{\@@citephrase{)}}}.})

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\Bigg(\Delta_{n}\cdot\sigma_{(l,0)}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{1}{\kappa}\Bigg(\frac{2(\exp{(-2u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})}-2\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})}+1)}{u^{4}\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2(\exp{(-2u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})}-2\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})}+1)}{u^{4}\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})}}\Bigg)\cdot 1_{\{b\in(0,1/2]\}}+\kappa T\cdot\Bigg(\frac{4(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2})}{3}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4(\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}})^{2}((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i-}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i-}^{n}})^{2})}{3}\Bigg)\cdot 1_{\{b\in[1/2,1)\}}\Bigg)\Bigg]\Bigg)+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle(\text{Using the boundedness of $\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}$ and the condition \eqref{cond-vov}.})

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\Bigg(\Delta_{n}\cdot\sigma_{(l,0)}^{2}\cdot\Bigg(\frac{2}{\kappa}\Bigg(\frac{2(\exp{(-2u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{(l,0)})}-2\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{(l,0)})}+1)}{u^{4}\exp{(-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{(l,0)})}}\Bigg)\cdot 1_{\{b\in(0,1/2]\}}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\kappa T\cdot\Bigg(\frac{4(\sigma_{(l,0)})^{2}((\tilde{\sigma}_{(l,0)}^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{(l,0)})^{2})}{3}\Bigg)\cdot 1_{\{b\in[1/2,1)\}}\Bigg)\Bigg)+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{p}Var(U|\mathcal{F}).

This finishes the proof of (
105
). For (
106
), we decompose

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r)}\right)\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\left(\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{3}\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\left(\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\sum_{r_{3}}\left(\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{3})}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\sum_{r_{3}}\sum_{r_{4}}\left(\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\right)^{4}\left(s^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}s^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}s^{n}_{(l,r_{3})}s^{n}_{(l,r_{4})}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle:=S_{i}^{n}(1)+S_{i}^{n}(2)+S_{i}^{n}(3)+S_{i}^{n}(4)+S_{i}^{n}(5),

where
r_{1}<r_{2}<r_{3}<r_{4}
and
r_{1},r_{2},r_{3},r_{4}=k_{n}+1,...,(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}
. By following the proof of (
105
), we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r)}\right)^{2}\right]=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}),\ \mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r)}\right)^{4}\right]=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{2}),

thus
S_{i}^{n}(1)=O_{p}(\Delta_{n})
. Since

\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{3}\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}-r_{1}\leq 2k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{3}\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}-r_{1}>2k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{3}\right]\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right)\right]

(By applying Holder’s inequality and using the results in Lemma
2
.)

\displaystyle\leq C\tilde{m}k_{n}\cdot k_{n}\cdot\Delta_{n}^{2}+C(\tilde{m}k_{n})^{2}\cdot\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot\Delta_{n}h_{n},

thus,
S_{i}^{n}(2)=O_{p}\left(\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}+\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}h_{n}\right)
.
Similarly, by decomposing the summation into the cases of
r_{k+1}-r_{k}\leq 2k_{n}
and
r_{k+1}-r_{k}>2k_{n}
for
k=1,2,3
, using successive conditioning and using Holder’s inequality, we can obtain

\displaystyle S_{i}^{n}(3)=O_{p}\left(\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\right),\qquad S_{i}^{n}(4)=O_{p}\left((\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{2}h_{n}^{2}+\tilde{m}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{2}\right),

\displaystyle S_{i}^{n}(5)=O_{p}\left((\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{3}h_{n}^{4}+(\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})h_{n}+(\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n})(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{2}h_{n}^{2}\right).

This finishes the proof of (
106
).
For (
107
), with
M=B+B^{\prime}+W
, similarly to the proof of (
96
), for
j=i-k_{n},...,n
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\cdot\sum_{j=i-k_{n}}^{n}\Delta_{j}^{n}M\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}s^{n}_{i}\cdot\sum_{j=i-k_{n}}^{i+k_{n}+1}\Delta_{j}^{n}M\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}M+\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+k_{n}+1}\Delta_{j}^{n}M\right)\cdot\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}M+\sum_{j=i-k_{n}}^{i-1}\Delta_{j}^{n}M\right)\cdot\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Delta_{i+}^{n}X\Delta_{i}^{n}M\cdot\left(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(1-2b)}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sqrt{\kappa T}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(2b-1)}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{i+}^{n}B\Delta_{i}^{n}M\bigg|\left(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}},\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\right)\right]\cdot\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\left(\Delta_{i+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{i+}^{n}B\right)\Delta_{i}^{n}M\cdot\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Delta_{i+}^{n}X\cdot\left(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(1-2b)}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sqrt{\kappa T}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(2b-1)}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\Delta_{j}^{n}M\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{i+}^{n}B\bigg|\left(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}},\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\right)\right]\cdot\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+k_{n}+1}\Delta_{j}^{n}M\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\left(\Delta_{i+}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{i+}^{n}B\right)\cdot\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\cdot\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+k_{n}+1}\Delta_{j}^{n}M\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\left(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(1-2b)}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sqrt{\kappa T}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(2b-1)}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\cdot\Bigg|\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X,\Delta_{i}^{n}M\right)\Bigg]\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X\Delta_{i}^{n}M\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\right)\cdot\Delta_{i}^{n}M\cdot\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\right)\cdot\Delta_{i}^{n}M\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\left(\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u)\right)\sum_{j=i-k_{n}}^{i-1}\Delta_{i}^{n}M\right)\cdot\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-}^{n}\left[\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right]\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\left(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(1-2b)}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sqrt{\kappa T}\sqrt{n}^{0\wedge(2b-1)}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\cdot\Delta_{j}^{n}M\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\cdot\Bigg|\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X,\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}},\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\Bigg]\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\left(\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime}(u)\right)\cdot\sum_{j=i-k_{n}}^{i-1}\Delta_{j}^{n}M\cdot\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\sigma_{t_{i-}^{n}}\Delta_{i}^{n}B\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle(\text{According to \eqref{spot-main}, \eqref{X-main}, the proof of \eqref{xi-23-W}, $\mathbb{E}[\Delta_{i}^{n}X]=O_{p}(\Delta_{n})$, and Holder's inequality.})

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}\cdot(h_{n}+\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}})\right).

Thus

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\xi(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}\cdot\left(\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}B+\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}B^{\prime}+\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}W\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}s^{n}_{(l,r)}\cdot\left(\sum_{r=1}^{(\tilde{m}+2)k_{n}}\left(\Delta_{(l,r)}^{n}B+\Delta_{(l,r)}^{n}B^{\prime}+\Delta_{(l,r)}^{n}W\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(h_{n}+\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\right),

which implies (
107
). This finishes the proof of (
102
) and (
23
).

The consistency result (
22
) is implied by (
23
). Moreover, if we fix
b=\frac{1}{2}
, by repeating the above proof steps, we find that it requires

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}}\left(n\cdot\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}h_{n}\right)\rightarrow 0,

which holds when
\max\{\beta,r\}\leq\frac{4}{3}
.

The proof of Theorem
1
is completed.
\square

8.3
Proof for Theorem
2

For
i=k_{n}+1,...,n-k_{n}
, we define

\displaystyle\begin{split}a_{i}^{n}&=\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\Bigg(\frac{3\Delta_{n}}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}}h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})-4(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2})\Delta_{n}\Bigg)\\
&:=a_{i}^{n}(1)+a_{i}^{n}(2)+a_{i}^{n}(3),\end{split}

(108)

with

\displaystyle a_{i}^{n}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\Bigg(\frac{3\Delta_{n}}{2}\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\qquad-\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad-\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})\Delta_{n}-4(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2})\Delta_{n}\Bigg)

\displaystyle a_{i}^{n}(2)

\displaystyle=\frac{3\Delta_{n}}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\qquad-\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)\Bigg)\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)\right)

\displaystyle a_{i}^{n}(3)

\displaystyle=\frac{3\Delta_{n}}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)\right)^{2},

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i\pm}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-b_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}(u)\right)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i\pm}}\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)-\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i\pm}}\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u),\end{split}

(109)

and
\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)
and
\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)
are defined as in (
84
). From (
86
), we have

\displaystyle\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)=O_{p}\left(h_{n}\right).

(110)

According to (
83
), Lemma 2 and Lemma 6 in
Liu et al. 2018
, with the boundedness of
\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}
and the condition (
9
), we can obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)&=\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)+O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}+\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1}{(\beta+\epsilon)}-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right)\\
\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)&=\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j)}(u)+O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\right),\end{split}

(111)

where

\displaystyle\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)

\displaystyle=\frac{-2\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right]\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}}u^{2}f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(u)},

\displaystyle\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j)}(u)

\displaystyle=\frac{(i+k_{n}-j)\left(2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}.

Moreover, by following the calculations in Lemma 7 of
Liu et al. 2018
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[(\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u))^{2}\right]=\frac{1}{k_{n}}h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}^{2}),\qquad\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[(\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u))^{4}\right]=\frac{1}{k_{n}^{2}}h_{3}(u,\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}^{2}),\\
&\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[(\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u))^{2}\frac{\left(2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)^{2}}{\Delta_{n}}\right]\\
&:=\frac{1}{k_{n}}h_{4}(u,\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}^{2})=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\right),\end{split}

(112)

with

\displaystyle h_{3}(u,\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}^{2}):=\frac{2f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(4u)-16f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(\sqrt{10}u)+48f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(\sqrt{6}u)-40f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(2u)+2}{u^{8}f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(2u)},

since

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right]\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right)^{4}\right]-4\cdot f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(u)\cdot\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right)^{3}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+6\cdot f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(\sqrt{2}u)\cdot\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right)^{2}\right]-3\cdot f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(2u)

(Using
(\cos{x})^{2}=\frac{\cos{2x}+1}{2}
,
(\cos{x})^{3}=\frac{\cos{3x}+3\cos{x}}{4}
and
(\cos{x})^{4}=\frac{\cos{4x}+4\cos{2x}+1}{8}
.)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(4u)-f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(\sqrt{10}u)+3f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(\sqrt{6}u)-\frac{5}{2}f^{(1)}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n},n}(2u)+\frac{1}{8},

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[(\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u))^{2}\frac{\left(2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)^{2}}{\Delta_{n}}\right]

(By Holder’s inequality.)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[(\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u))^{4}\right]\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\frac{\left(2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)^{2}}{\Delta_{n}}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{k_{n}}\left(27h_{3}(u,\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}^{2})(h_{2}(\sigma_{t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}}^{2}))^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

Furthermore, by following the proof of Proposition
1
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Bigg(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j)}(u)\right)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]=\frac{1}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}(u,t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n})+h_{2}(t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}),

(113)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Bigg(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j)}(u)\right)\Bigg)^{4}\Bigg]\\
&\text{(Noticing that the functions $x(\cos{(cx)})^{k}$ and $x^{k}\cos{(cx)}$, for any constant $c$ and $k\in\mathbb{N}^{+},$ are odd.)}\\
&=\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)\Bigg)^{4}+12\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}\in I^{n}_{i+}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j_{1})}(u)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j_{2})}(u)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg)\Bigg]\\
&\quad+\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[6\sum_{j_{1}\in I^{n}_{i+}}\sum_{j_{2}\in I^{n}_{i+}}\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j_{1})}(u)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg(\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j_{2})}(u)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg)\Bigg]\\
&\quad+\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\Bigg(\Bigg(\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)^{4}+12\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}\in I^{n}_{i+}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg(\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j_{1})}(u)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg(\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j_{2})}(u)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg)\Bigg]\\
&=\frac{12}{k_{n}^{4}\Delta_{n}^{2}}(h_{1}(u,t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}))^{2}+\frac{6}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}(u,t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n})h_{2}(t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n})+12(h_{2}(t_{(i-k_{n}-1)}^{n}))^{2}+O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\right).\end{split}

(114)

Similarly, we can prove the same results when
j\in I^{n}_{i-}
.

Before presenting the proof of Theorem
2
, we give the following lemmas to be used and their proofs.

Lemma 3

For
j\in I_{i+}^{n}\cup I_{i-}^{n}
, we have

\displaystyle f^{(1)}_{t_{j}^{n},n}(u)

\displaystyle=f^{(1)}_{t_{i}^{n},n}(u)+O_{p}\left((k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}\right),

(115)

\displaystyle h_{1}(u,t_{j}^{n})

\displaystyle=h_{1}(u,t_{i}^{n})+O_{p}\left((k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}\right),

(116)

\displaystyle h_{2}(t_{j}^{n})

\displaystyle=h_{2}(t_{i}^{n})+O_{p}(\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}).

(117)

Proof of Lemma
3
:
After applying It
\hat{\text{o}}
’s lemma with (
7
), we have
