\displaystyle d\exp{\left(\frac{-u^{2}(\sigma^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i}^{n}})}{2}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{-u^{2}}{2}\exp{\left(\frac{-u^{2}(\sigma^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i}^{n}})}{2}\right)}\left(2\sigma_{s}\tilde{b}_{s}+(\tilde{\sigma}_{s})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{s})^{2}\right)+\frac{u^{4}}{2}\exp{\left(\frac{-u^{2}(\sigma^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i}^{n}})}{2}\right)}((\tilde{\sigma}_{s})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{s})^{2})\sigma_{s}^{2}\right)ds

\displaystyle\qquad-u^{2}\exp{\left(\frac{-u^{2}(\sigma^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i}^{n}})}{2}\right)}\sigma_{s}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}dB_{s}-u^{2}\exp{\left(\frac{-u^{2}(\sigma^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i}^{n}})}{2}\right)}\sigma_{s}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{s}dB^{\prime}_{s}.

Since
\tilde{b},\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}
are bounded and with condition (
9
), by using It
\hat{\text{o}}
’s Isometry, we can get

\displaystyle f^{(1)}_{t_{j}^{n},n}(u)-f^{(1)}_{t_{i}^{n},n}(u)

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(\frac{-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i}^{n}}}{2}\right)}\left(\exp{\left(\frac{-u^{2}(\sigma^{2}_{t_{j}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i}^{n}})}{2}\right)}-1\right)

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(\frac{-u^{2}\sigma^{2}_{t_{i}^{n}}}{2}\right)}\cdot\int_{t_{i}^{n}}^{t_{j}^{n}}d\exp{\left(\frac{-u^{2}(\sigma^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i}^{n}})}{2}\right)}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}\right).

Furthermore, by directly using the above conclusion, we can obtain (
116
).
The result of (
117
) can be directly obtained from the boundedness of
\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}
and the condition (
9
).

\square

Lemma 4

Under the same assumptions and conditions as in Theorem
2
, for
i=k_{n}+1,...,n-k_{n}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}\right)\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+h_{n}^{2}\right)\right),

(118)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}\right)^{2}\right]&=\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\left(\frac{117}{2}\frac{(h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}))^{2}}{k_{n}^{4}\Delta_{n}^{2}}+36\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n})h_{2}(t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+\frac{117}{2}(h_{2}(t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}))^{2}\right)\\
&+O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}h_{n}}{k_{n}}\right),\end{split}

(119)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}\right)^{3}\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{k_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right),\ \mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}\right)^{4}\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{2}}{k_{n}^{2}}\right),\ \mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}\right)^{m}\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{m/2}}{k_{n}^{m/2}}\right).

(120)

Furthermore, for
j=k_{n}+1,...,n-k_{n}
with
i<j
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}a^{n}_{j}\right)\right]\leq C\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}},

(121)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}\cdot a^{n}_{j}\right)\right]=\left(\frac{9}{2}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{197}{40}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{153}{35}h_{2}^{2}\right)\Delta_{n}+O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\Delta_{n}h_{n}),

(122)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{j=i+k_{n}+1}^{i+2k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}\cdot a^{n}_{j}\right)\right]=\left(\frac{9}{4}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{63}{16}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{45}{28}h_{2}^{2}\right)\Delta_{n}+O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\Delta_{n}h_{n}),

(123)

and, if
j-i>2k_{n}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}\cdot a^{n}_{j}\right)\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+h_{n}^{2}\right)^{2}\right).

(124)

Proof of Lemma
4
:
For (
118
), we write
a_{i}^{n}=a_{i}^{n}(1)+a_{i}^{n}(2)+a_{i}^{n}(3)
.
According to the proof of Lemma
1
and the results in Lemma
3
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}(1)\right)\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}\right)\right),

(125)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}(2)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{3\Delta_{n}}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad-\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)\Bigg)\Bigg|\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u),\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)\right)\Bigg]\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\equiv 0.

We have
\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}(3)\right)\right]=O_{p}(\frac{h_{n}^{2}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}})
since
\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)=O_{p}\left(h_{n}\right)
. These results together yield (
118
).

For (
119
), from the proof of (
118
), we have

\displaystyle a^{n}_{i}(3)=O_{p}\left(\frac{h_{n}^{2}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\right),\qquad a^{n}_{i}(2)=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}h_{n}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\right),

(126)

since by Holder’s inequality and using (
125
), we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left|a^{n}_{i}(2)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad-\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]\cdot\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)\right)^{2}\right]\Bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}h_{n}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}(1)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{3}{2}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)-\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})-4(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2})\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

(According to (
111
).)

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{3}{2}\Bigg(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j)}(u)\right)-\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}}^{2})-4(\sigma_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})^{2})\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]+O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{k_{n}}\right)

(By following the calculation in the proof of Lemma

After summing up the above results, we can obtain

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(\Big(\frac{(k_{n}-k)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}-k}s^{2}\Big)\Big(\frac{(k_{n}-k)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}-k}(k+s)^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}-k}\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}(k+s)s}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big)\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(\frac{(k-1)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{j=k_{n}-k+2}^{k_{n}}s^{2}\Big)\Big)\Big(\frac{(k_{n}-k)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{j=k+1}^{k_{n}}s^{2}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(\frac{(k-1)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}s^{2}\Big)\Big)\Big(\frac{(k_{n}-k)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}-k}s^{2}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad-4\Big(\frac{(k-1)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}(k_{n}+1-k+s)s\Big)\Big)\Big(\frac{(k_{n}-k)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}-k}(k+s)s\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{(2k_{n}-k-1)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}-k}(s^{2})+\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}}(s^{2})-(k_{n}-k+1)^{2}\Big)\Big)\Big(\frac{(k+1)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{k}(s^{2})+k^{2}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(\frac{(k+1)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{s=k_{n}+1-k}^{k_{n}}(s^{2})+(k_{n}-k+1)^{2}\Big)\Big)\Bigg(\frac{2}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}+2h_{2}\Bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\Bigg(\frac{3h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+3h_{2}\Bigg)^{2}+O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{k_{n}}+\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}h_{n}\right)\Bigg)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{9\Delta_{n}}{4}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{1}{3}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{13}{20}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{17}{70}h_{2}^{2}\Big)+2\Big(\frac{1}{3}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{11}{20}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{33}{140}h_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{1}{2}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{3}{4}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{1}{4}h_{2}^{2}\Big)+4\Big(\frac{1}{6}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{7}{20}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{103}{560}h_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{1}{2}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{5}{4}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{3}{4}h_{2}^{2}\Big)+\Big(\frac{1}{3}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{13}{20}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{17}{70}h_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+2\Big(\frac{1}{3}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{33}{140}h_{2}^{2}\Big)+\Big(\frac{1}{3}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{13}{20}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{17}{70}h_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+2\Big(\frac{1}{3}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{11}{20}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{33}{140}h_{2}^{2}\Big)+\Big(\frac{1}{6}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{3}{5}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{71}{140}h_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{1}{6}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{1}{10}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{1}{140}h_{2}^{2}\Big)-4\Big(\frac{1}{6}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{1}{5}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{29}{560}h_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{2}{3}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{17}{20}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{9}{35}h_{2}^{2}\Big)+\Big(2\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+2h_{2}\Big)\Big(\frac{1}{2}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+\frac{3}{4}h_{2}\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-\Delta_{n}\Bigg(\frac{3h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+3h_{2}\Bigg)^{2}+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\Delta_{n}h_{n}+\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{9}{2}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{197}{40}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{153}{35}h_{2}^{2}\right)\Delta_{n}+O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\Delta_{n}h_{n}).

For (
123
), from the proof of (
122
), we only need to calculate
\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}(1)\cdot a^{n}_{i+k}(1)\right)\right]
, for
k=k_{n}+1,...,2k_{n}
and sum them up, while the remaining terms are negligible. Similarly, we define

\displaystyle I_{i}^{n}(1)=\{i-k_{n},...,i-1\},\ I_{i}^{n}(2)=\{i+1,...,i+k-k_{n}\},\ I_{i}^{n}(3)=\{i+k-k_{n}+1,...,i+k_{n}\},

\displaystyle I_{i}^{n}(4)=\{i+k_{n}+1,...,i+k-1\},\ I_{i}^{n}(5)=\{i+k+1,...,i+k+k_{n}\},

and

\displaystyle A_{i,n}^{+}(j)=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j)}(u)\right),\quad A_{i,n}^{-}(j)=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)

\displaystyle B_{i,n}^{+}(j)=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i+k,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{i+k,n}^{{}^{\prime}(3,j)}(u)\right),\quad B_{i,n}^{-}(j)=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\xi_{i+k,n}^{{}^{\prime}(2,j)}(u)+\xi_{t_{{(i+k)}-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right).

By following the proof of (
122
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}(1)\cdot a^{n}_{i+k}(1)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{9\Delta_{n}}{4k_{n}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\Bigg(\sum_{m=2,3}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i}(m)}\left(A_{i,n}^{+}(j)\right)-\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i}(1)}\left(A_{i,n}^{-}(j)\right)\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i}(5)}\left(B_{i,n}^{+}(j)\right)-\sum_{m=3,4}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i}(m)}\left(B_{i,n}^{-}(j)\right)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\Bigg(\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}}^{2})+4(\sigma_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})^{2})\Bigg)^{2}+O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{k_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{9\Delta_{n}}{4k_{n}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}\in I_{i}^{n}(3)}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}((A_{i,n}^{+}(j_{1}))^{2}(B_{i,n}^{-}(j_{2}))^{2})+\sum_{j\in I_{i}^{n}(3)}((A_{i,n}^{+}(j))^{2}(B_{i,n}^{-}(j))^{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}\in I_{i}^{n}(3)}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}((A_{i,n}^{+}(j_{1}))(A_{i,n}^{+}(j_{2})))(B_{i,n}^{-}(j_{1}))(B_{i,n}^{-}(j_{2}))

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\sum_{j\in I_{i}^{n}(3)}(A_{i,n}^{+}(j))^{2}\right)\cdot\left(\sum_{j\in I_{i}^{n}(5)}(B_{i,n}^{+}(j))^{2}+\sum_{j\in I_{i}^{n}(4)}(B_{i,n}^{-}(j))^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\sum_{j\in I_{i}^{n}(2)}(A_{i,n}^{+}(j))^{2}+\sum_{j\in I_{i}^{n}(1)}(A_{i,n}^{-}(j))^{2}\right)\cdot\left(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i}(5)}\left(B_{i,n}^{+}(j)\right)^{2}+\sum_{m=3,4}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i}(m)}\left(B_{i,n}^{-}(j)\right)^{2}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\Bigg(\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}}^{2})+4(\sigma_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})^{2})\Bigg)^{2}+O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{k_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{9\Delta_{n}}{4k_{n}}\cdot\Bigg(\Big(\Big(\frac{(2k_{n}-k)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}(2k_{n}-k-s+1)^{2}\Big)\Big(\frac{(2k_{n}-k)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}(k_{n}-s)^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}(2k_{n}-k-s+1)^{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big)\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}(k_{n}-s)^{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(\frac{(2k_{n}-k)h_{3}}{k_{n}^{2}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{h_{4}}{k_{n}^{4}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}(k_{n}-s)^{2}+\frac{4h_{4}}{k_{n}^{4}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}((k_{n}-s)(2k_{n}-k-s+1))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{h_{4}}{k_{n}^{4}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}(2k_{n}-k-s+1)^{2}+\frac{27h_{2}^{2}}{k_{n}^{6}}\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}((k_{n}-s)^{2}(2k_{n}-k-s+1)^{2})\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\Big(\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}((k_{n}-s)(2k_{n}-k-s+1))}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big)\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}\Big(\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}((k_{n}-s)(2k_{n}-k-s+1))}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big)^{2}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(\frac{(2k_{n}-k)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{2k_{n}-k}(2k_{n}-k-s+1)^{2}\Big)\Big(\frac{(2k_{n}-1)h_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}}s^{2}+\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}-1}s^{2}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(\frac{kh_{1}}{k_{n}(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})}+\frac{3h_{2}}{k_{n}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{k-k_{n}}(k_{n}+1-s)^{2}+\sum_{s=1}^{k_{n}}(k_{n}+1-s)^{2}\Big)\Big)\Bigg(\frac{2}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}+2h_{2}\Bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\Bigg(\frac{3h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+3h_{2}\Bigg)^{2}+O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{k_{n}}\right).

Summing up these results for
k=k_{n}+1,...,2k_{n}-1
yields

\displaystyle\sum_{j=i+k_{n}+1}^{i+2k_{n}-1}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a^{n}_{i}\cdot a^{n}_{j}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{9\Delta_{n}}{4}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{1}{3}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{13}{20}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{17}{70}h_{2}^{2}\Big)+2\Big(\frac{1}{3}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{11}{20}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{33}{140}h_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{3}{2}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{1}{2}h_{2}^{2}\Big)+\Big(2\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+2h_{2}\Big)\Big(\frac{3}{2}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+\frac{7}{4}h_{2}\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-\Delta_{n}\Bigg(\frac{3h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+3h_{2}\Bigg)^{2}+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\Delta_{n}h_{n}+\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{9}{4}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{(k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n})^{2}}+\frac{63}{16}\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{2}+\frac{45}{28}h_{2}^{2}\right)\Delta_{n}+O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\Delta_{n}h_{n}).

The result of (
124
) can be directly obtained by successive conditioning, as used for proving (
97
) in Lemma
2
, with (
118
) since there is no overlapping terms between
a_{i}^{n}
and
a_{j}^{n}
.

This finishes the proof of Lemma
4
.

\square

Proof of Theorem
2
:

We focus on the proof of the central limit theorem result (
31
) for
b=\frac{1}{2}
and will show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\widehat{VoV}_{[0,T]}-VoV_{[0,T]}\right)\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\cdot W,

(127)

with

\displaystyle\text{Var}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\cdot W|\mathcal{F}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{27}{2}\frac{(h_{1}(u,t,\sigma_{t}^{2}))^{2}}{\kappa^{4}}+\frac{709}{40}\frac{h_{1}(u,t,\sigma_{t}^{2})}{\kappa^{2}}h_{2}(t,\sigma_{t}^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2}))

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{837}{70}(h_{2}(t,\sigma_{t}^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2})))^{2}.

We decompose

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\widehat{VoV}_{[0,T]}-VoV_{[0,T]}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\frac{3}{2k_{n}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)^{2}-\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}}h_{1}(u,\widehat{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})-4(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2})\Delta_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(4(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2})\Delta_{n}\right)-\int_{0}^{T}4(\sigma_{t}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2})dt\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Big(\frac{3}{2k_{n}}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})\Delta_{n}-4(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2})\Delta_{n}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Big(\frac{3}{k_{n}}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\ \cdot\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\frac{3}{2k_{n}}\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(4(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2})\Delta_{n}\right)-\int_{0}^{T}4(\sigma_{t}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2})dt\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})-h_{1}(u,\widehat{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})\right)\Delta_{n}\right)

\displaystyle:=A_{i}^{n}+B_{i}^{n}+C_{i}^{n}+D_{i}^{n}+E_{i}^{n}.

Using the boundedness of
\sigma,\tilde{\sigma},\tilde{\sigma}
and the conditions (
9
), we can obtain

\displaystyle C_{i}^{n}=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\right),\quad D_{i}^{n}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\right),

thus,
C_{i}^{n}+D_{i}^{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0
.
For
B_{i}^{n}
, we define

\displaystyle B^{\prime n}_{i}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Big(\frac{3}{k_{n}}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})\right)\Big).

With the condition (
9
) and Lemma
1
, we can obtain
B^{n}_{i}-B^{\prime n}_{i}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1-\beta}{2}}}{k_{n}}\right)
. By following the proof in Lemma
2
(Directly replacing
\Delta_{i}^{n}X
in
s_{i}^{n}
with
\Delta_{i}^{n}\sigma^{2}
.), we can obtain
\mathbb{E}[B^{\prime n}_{i}]=O_{p}(\frac{1}{k_{n}})
and
\mathbb{E}[(B^{\prime n}_{i})^{2}]=O_{p}(\frac{1}{k_{n}^{2}})
. These results and Chebyshev’s inequality imply that
B_{i}^{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0
.
According to Lemma
3
, we have
E_{i}^{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0
since
E_{i}^{n}=O_{p}((k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{1/4})
.
Thus, it remains to show

\displaystyle A_{i}^{n}\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\cdot W.

(128)

We write
A_{i}^{n}=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}a_{i}^{n}
with
a_{i}^{n}
defined in (
108
).
As in the proof of Theorem
1
, we split the sum over
i
into big blocks of size
(\tilde{m}+2)k_{n}
with
\tilde{m}
satisfying

\displaystyle\tilde{m}\rightarrow\infty,\quad\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\rightarrow 0.

(129)

We define
I(\tilde{m},n,l)=(l-1)(\tilde{m}+2)k_{n}+1
for
l=1,...,l_{n}(\tilde{m})
with the total number of big blocks
l_{n}(\tilde{m})=\lfloor\frac{\lfloor T/\Delta_{n}\rfloor-1}{(\tilde{m}+2)k_{n}}\rfloor
. In this way, in the
l
-th big block, for
t_{i}^{n}
with
I(\tilde{m},n,l)+k_{n}+1\leq i\leq I(\tilde{m},n,l)+(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}
, the increments of
\Delta_{j}^{n}X
constituting
a_{i}^{n}
are within
I(\tilde{m},n,l)
, making
a_{i}^{n}
be
\mathcal{F}_{I(\tilde{m},n,l+1)\Delta_{n}}
-measurable. For simplicity, we use
(l,j)
for the time point
(I(\tilde{m},n,l)+j)\Delta_{n}
or its index
(I(\tilde{m},n,l)+j)
and
\mathbb{E}^{n}_{(l,j)}[\cdot]
for the conditional expectation
\mathbb{E}[\cdot|\mathcal{F}_{(l,j)}]
, with
l=1,...,l_{n}(\tilde{m})
and
j
being any integer number.
We decompose
\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}a_{i}^{n}=W(\tilde{m})^{n}+\tilde{W}(\tilde{m})^{n}+\tilde{W}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}
with

\displaystyle\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}=\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}a^{n}_{(l,r)},\quad\tilde{\zeta}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}=\sum_{r=-k_{n}}^{k_{n}}a^{n}_{(l,r)},

\displaystyle W(\tilde{m})^{n}=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n},\quad\tilde{W}(\tilde{m})^{n}=\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\tilde{\zeta}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n},\quad\tilde{W}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}=\sum_{i=(l_{n}(\tilde{m})+1,-k_{n})}^{n-k_{n}}a^{n}_{i}.

To prove (
128
), it is sufficient to show that

\displaystyle\tilde{W}(\tilde{m})^{n}+\tilde{W}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(130)

\displaystyle W(\tilde{m})^{n}\longrightarrow^{L_{s}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\cdot W.

(131)

For (
130
), we notice that whenever
\tilde{m}>2
,
\tilde{\zeta}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}
is
(l+1,-2k_{n}-1)
-measurable and there is no overlapping terms among the sequence of
\{\tilde{\zeta}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}:l=2,...,l_{n}(\tilde{m})\}
. According to Lemma 4.1 in
Jacod 2012
,
\tilde{W}(\tilde{m})^{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0
can be proved by showing

\displaystyle\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,-2k_{n}-1)}^{n}[\tilde{\zeta}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}]\longrightarrow^{p}0,\qquad\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,-2k_{n}-1)}^{n}[(\tilde{\zeta}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}]\longrightarrow^{p}0.

(132)

For
r=-k_{n},...,k_{n}
with
l=2,...,l_{n}(\tilde{m})
, according to Lemma
4
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,-2k_{n}-1)}^{n}\left[a^{n}_{(l,r)}\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+h_{n}^{2}\right)\right).

(133)

When
\beta<3/2
and
r<1
, since
\tilde{m}\rightarrow\infty
and
n^{1/2}/k_{n}=O(1)
, we have

\displaystyle l_{n}(\tilde{m})k_{n}\cdot\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+h_{n}^{2}\right)\right)\rightarrow 0,

thus,
\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,-2k_{n}-1)}^{n}[\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}]\longrightarrow^{p}0
. Observe

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,-k_{n}-1)}^{n}[(\tilde{\zeta}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(l,-k_{n}-1)}^{n}\left[\sum_{r=-k_{n}}^{k_{n}}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r)}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{(l,-k_{n}-1)}^{n}\left[2\sum_{r_{1},r_{2}=-k_{n}}^{k_{n}}\sum_{r_{1}<r_{2}<r_{1}+2k_{n}+1}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\cdot a^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{(l,-k_{n}-1)}^{n}\left[2\sum_{r_{1},r_{2}=-k_{n}}^{k_{n}}\sum_{r_{1}+2k_{n}+1<r_{2}}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\cdot a^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right]

\displaystyle(\text{According to Lemma \ref {lem-vov-2}.})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(k_{n}\Delta_{n}),

as
\tilde{m}\rightarrow\infty
, we have
\sum_{l=2}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,-k_{n}-1)}^{n}[(\tilde{\xi}(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}]\longrightarrow^{p}0
.
As for
\tilde{Z}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}\longrightarrow^{p}0
, similarly, by following the above proof, we can prove

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{Z}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n}]=\tilde{m}k_{n}\cdot O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+h_{n}^{2}\right)\right),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{Z}^{\prime}(\tilde{m})^{n})^{2}]=o_{p}\left(\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\right),

Since
\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\rightarrow 0
, Chebyshev’s inequality implies the convergence in probability. This finishes the proof of (
130
).

For (
131
), from the above proof of (
130
), if
\beta<3/2
and
r<1
, since
k_{n}\Delta_{n}\rightarrow 0
and
n^{1/2}/k_{n}=O(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}\right]=n\cdot O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+h_{n}^{2}\right)\right)\longrightarrow^{p}0,

and it is obvious that
\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}
is
\mathcal{F}_{(l+1,0)}
-measurable, thus
\left\{\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n},\mathcal{F}_{(l+1,0)}\right\}
behaves like a martingale difference array. According to Theorem 2.2.15 in
Jacod and Protter 2012
, it remains to show

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[(\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}\text{Var}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\cdot W|\mathcal{F}\right),

(134)

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[(\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{4}\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(135)

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}\cdot\left(\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\right)\right]\longrightarrow^{p}0,

(136)

where
\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M:=M_{(l+1,0)}-M_{(l,0)}
for
M=B,B^{\prime}
or
W
with
W
being a bounded martingale orthogonal to both
B
and
B^{\prime}
.

For (
134
), we decompose

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[(\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[a^{n}_{(l,r)}\cdot a^{n}_{(l,j)}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[(a^{n}_{(l,r)})^{2}]+\sum_{r,j=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\sum_{|j-r|\leq 2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[a^{n}_{(l,r)}a^{n}_{(l,j)}]+\sum_{r,j=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\sum_{|j-r|>2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}[a^{n}_{(l,r)}a^{n}_{(l,j)}]

\displaystyle:=H(\tilde{m},1)_{l}^{n}+H(\tilde{m},2)_{l}^{n}+H(\tilde{m},3)_{l}^{n}.

According to Lemma
4
, when
\beta<3/2
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}H(\tilde{m},1)_{l}^{n}+\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}H(\tilde{m},3)_{l}^{n}=O_{p}\left(\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}+\frac{1}{k_{n}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}H(\tilde{m},2)_{l}^{n}=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}(H(u,(l,0))\cdot\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n})+O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}}+h_{n}\right)\longrightarrow^{p}\frac{Var(W|\mathcal{F})}{\kappa}.\end{split}

(137)

For (
135
), we decompose

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r)}\right)\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{4}\right]+C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\left(\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{3}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\left(\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{2}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\sum_{r_{3}}\left(\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{2}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{3})}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\sum_{r_{3}}\sum_{r_{4}}\left(\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}a^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}a^{n}_{(l,r_{3})}a^{n}_{(l,r_{4})}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle:=S_{i}^{n}(1)+S_{i}^{n}(2)+S_{i}^{n}(3)+S_{i}^{n}(4)+S_{i}^{n}(5),

where
r_{1}<r_{2}<r_{3}<r_{4}
and
r_{1},r_{2},r_{3},r_{4}=k_{n}+1,...,(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}
.
According to Lemma
4
, we have
S_{i}^{n}(1)=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}^{2}}\right)
.
Since

\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{3}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}-r_{1}\leq 2k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{3}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right)\right]+\sum_{r_{1}}\sum_{r_{2}-r_{1}>2k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{1})}\right)^{3}\right]\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(a^{n}_{(l,r_{2})}\right)\right)\right]

(By using the results in Lemma
4
and Holder’s inequality.)

\displaystyle\leq C\tilde{m}k_{n}\cdot k_{n}\cdot\frac{\Delta_{n}^{2}}{k_{n}^{2}}+C(\tilde{m}k_{n})^{2}\cdot\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{k_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+h_{n}^{2}\right)\right),

thus,

\displaystyle S_{i}^{n}(2)=O_{p}\left(\frac{\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}^{2}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+h_{n}^{2}\right)\right).

Similarly, by decomposing the summation into the cases of
r_{k+1}-r_{k}\leq 2k_{n}
and
r_{k+1}-r_{k}>2k_{n}
for
k=1,2,3
, we can obtain

\displaystyle S_{i}^{n}(3)=O_{p}\left(\frac{\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}^{2}}\right),\qquad S_{i}^{n}(4)=O_{p}\left(\frac{\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle S_{i}^{n}(5)=O_{p}\left((\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{1}{2}}+(\tilde{m}k_{n}\Delta_{n})(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+k_{n}\Delta_{n}\right).

This finishes the proof of (
135
).
For (
136
), with
M=B+B^{\prime}+W
, similarly to the proof of (
118
), we decompose

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}a^{n}_{(l,r)}\cdot\left(\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}\left(a^{n}_{(l,r)}(1)+a^{n}_{(l,r)}(2)+a^{n}_{(l,r)}(3)\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\right)\right],

where
a^{n}_{(l,r)}(1),a^{n}_{(l,r)}(2),a^{n}_{(l,r)}(3)
are defined when proving (
118
). And we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(a^{n}_{(l,r)}(3)\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\Bigg(\frac{3\Delta_{n}}{2}\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\qquad-\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad-\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}(u,\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})\Delta_{n}-4(\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})((\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2}+(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}})^{2})\Delta_{n}\Bigg)\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\Bigg[\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\qquad-\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)\Bigg)^{2}\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{k_{n}}\right),

since

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\Bigg[\Bigg(\Bigg(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\Bigg)^{2}\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\cdot\left(W_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-W_{t_{i}^{n}}+B_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B_{t_{i}^{n}}+B^{\prime}_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B^{\prime}_{t_{i}^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{2}{k_{n}u^{4}(f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{2u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cos\left(\frac{2u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{2}{\sqrt{k_{n}}u^{2}(f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\sin\left(\frac{2u(\sigma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\sin\left(\frac{2u(\gamma_{t_{j-1}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}L)}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle(\text{Similar to the proof of \eqref{xi-23-W}.})

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\Bigg[\Bigg(\Bigg(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\Bigg)^{2}\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\cdot\left(W_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-W_{t_{i}^{n}}+B_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B_{t_{i}^{n}}+B^{\prime}_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B^{\prime}_{t_{i}^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)^{2}\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{(i+k_{n}+1-j)^{2}}{k_{n}^{2}k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)^{2}\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\equiv 0,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\Bigg[\Bigg(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\Bigg)\Bigg(\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\cdot\left(W_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-W_{t_{i}^{n}}+B_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B_{t_{i}^{n}}+B^{\prime}_{t_{i+k_{n}}^{n}}-B^{\prime}_{t_{i}^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)\right)\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\right)\cdot\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\frac{-2(i+k_{n}+1-j)}{k_{n}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\sqrt{k_{n}}u^{2}f_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u)}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\Bigg[\left(\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{j-1}^{n}\left[\cos\left(\frac{u\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\left(2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B+2\sigma_{t_{i+}^{n}}\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t_{i+}^{n}}\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}W+\Delta_{j}^{n}B+\Delta_{j}^{n}B^{\prime}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}},

and similar results can be obtained for other terms.
And

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(a^{n}_{(l,r)}(2)\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\Bigg(\frac{3\Delta_{n}}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i+}}\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}}\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(2,j)}(u)+\sum_{j\in I^{n}_{i-}}\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{(3,j)}(u)\Bigg)\Bigg)\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\Bigg)\Bigg|(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u),\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u))\Bigg]\cdot

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)\right)\Bigg]

(By using the results in the proof of (
82
),
\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)=O_{p}(h_{n})
and applying Holder’s inequality.)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}h_{n}),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(a^{n}_{(l,r)}(3)\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\left(\frac{3\Delta_{n}}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\xi_{t_{i+}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)-\xi_{t_{i-}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)\right)^{2}\cdot\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\right)\right]

(By using
\xi_{t_{i\pm}^{n},n}^{\prime\prime}(u)=O_{p}(h_{n})
and applying Holder’s inequality.)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}\sqrt{\tilde{m}}(h_{n})^{2}).

With these results, we can obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\zeta(\tilde{m})_{l}^{n}\cdot\left(\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{n}(\tilde{m})}\mathbb{E}_{(l,0)}\left[\sum_{r=k_{n}+1}^{(\tilde{m}+1)k_{n}}a^{n}_{(l,r)}\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{2\tilde{m}k_{n}}\left(\Delta_{l,\tilde{m}}^{n}M\right)\right]

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(h_{n}+\sqrt{\tilde{m}}(h_{n})^{2}\right),

which implies the result of (
136
). This ends the proof of (
131
) and (
127
).

The result for the case where
\frac{1}{2}<b<1
can be directly obtained from the above result with
\kappa=\infty
. Plugging
k_{n}=\lfloor\kappa n^{b}\rfloor
into (
127
) finishes the proof of (
31
).

For the consistency result (
30
), by following the above proof, we find that it requires

\displaystyle\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\cdot n\cdot\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\left(u^{2}(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{\frac{3}{4}}+|u|^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}}+h_{n}^{2}\right)\right)\longrightarrow 0,

which always holds when
\beta<2
and
r<4/3
.

The proof of Theorem
2
is completed.

\square

8.4
Proofs for Section
3.4
and
3.5

Proof of Theorem
3
:

According to Theorem 1 in
Liu et al. 2018
, we have
\widehat{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\longrightarrow^{p}\sigma_{t}^{2}
, thus
g(\widehat{\sigma}_{t}^{2})\longrightarrow^{p}g(\sigma_{t}^{2})
. Noticing that
\widehat{\sigma}_{t}^{2},\sigma_{t}^{2}
are bounded almost surely, and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{I}(g)-I(g)\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i=0}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\int_{t_{i}^{n}}^{t_{i+1}^{n}}\left(g(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t})-g(\sigma_{t}^{2})\right)dt\right)-\int_{t_{n-k_{n}+1}^{n}}^{T}g(\sigma_{t}^{2})dt\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\int_{t_{i}^{n}}^{t_{i+1}^{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left(g(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t})-g(\sigma_{t}^{2})\right)\right|\right]dt\right)+Ck_{n}\Delta_{n},

the conclusion then follows from the dominant convergence theorem and Chebyshev’s theorem. The proof is completed.

\square

Proof of Theorem
4
:
We focus on the discussion for
b=\frac{1}{2}
, of which the case where
b>\frac{1}{2}
is a direct result. We first show that

\displaystyle\widehat{H_{n}^{3}}=\frac{n}{k_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)^{4}\longrightarrow^{p}\int_{0}^{T}\left(\frac{135}{2}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{\kappa^{4}}+38\frac{h_{1}h_{2}}{\kappa^{2}}+\frac{135}{2}(h_{2})^{2}\right)dt.

(138)

We decompose

\displaystyle\frac{n}{k_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)^{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{k_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{n}{k_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Big(4\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\ \cdot\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{n}{k_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Big(4\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\ \cdot\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)^{3}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{n}{k_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Big(6\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\ \cdot\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{n}{k_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)^{4}

\displaystyle:=P_{i}^{n,1}+P_{i}^{n,2}+P_{i}^{n,3}+P_{i}^{n,4}+P_{i}^{n,5}.

For
P_{i}^{n,1}
, we rewrite

\displaystyle P_{i}^{n,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Bigg(\left(\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}}\left(\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}-\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}}\left(\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}-\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}}\left(\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}-\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle:=\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}p_{i}^{n,1}(j)+\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\overline{p_{i}^{n,1}}(j).

Since

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}p_{i}^{n,1}(j)\right]=\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}\left[p_{i}^{n,1}(j)\right]\right]\equiv 0,

by applying Holder’s inequality with Lemma
4
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}p_{i}^{n,1}(j)\right)^{2}\right]=\frac{16k_{n}^{2}}{81}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\sum_{|j_{2}-j_{1}|\leq k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[p_{i}^{n,1}(j_{1})p_{i}^{n,1}(j_{2})\right]\leq Ck_{n}\Delta_{n},

thus
\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}p_{i}^{n,1}(j)\longrightarrow^{p}0
.
And we have

\displaystyle\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\overline{p_{i}^{n,1}}(j)

\displaystyle=\frac{4k_{n}}{9}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a_{i}^{n}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}\Delta_{n}+3h_{2}\Delta_{n}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

(By using the results in Lemma
4
.)

\displaystyle=\frac{4\Delta_{n}}{9}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\Bigg(\left(\frac{117}{2}\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{k_{n}^{4}\Delta_{n}^{2}}+36\frac{h_{1}h_{2}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}+\frac{117}{2}(h_{2})^{2}\right)+\left(\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}+3h_{2}\right)^{2}\Bigg)\right]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{p}\int_{0}^{T}\left(30\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{\kappa^{4}}+24\frac{h_{1}h_{2}}{\kappa^{2}}+30(h_{2})^{2}\right)dt.

Thus

\displaystyle P_{i}^{n,1}\longrightarrow^{p}\int_{0}^{T}\left(30\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{\kappa^{4}}+24\frac{h_{1}h_{2}}{\kappa^{2}}+30(h_{2})^{2}\right)dt.

For
P_{i}^{n,2}
, by using Holder’s inequality with Lemma
4
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}+1}\Big[\Big|\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Big)\Big|\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}+1}\Big[\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{6}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}+1}\Big[\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Big)^{2}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=k_{n}^{9/4}\Delta_{n}^{3/4}\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}}\left(\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}-\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}+1}\Big[\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Big)^{2}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=k_{n}^{9/4}\Delta_{n}^{3/4}\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a_{i}^{n}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}\Delta_{n}+3h_{2}\Delta_{n}\right)\right)^{3}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}+1}\Big[\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Big)^{2}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq Ck_{n}^{3/2}\Delta_{n}^{2}.

Furthermore, we can get
\mathbb{E}[|P_{i}^{n,2}|]\leq C/\sqrt{k_{n}}
and
P_{i}^{n,2}\longrightarrow^{p}0
. Similarly, we can prove that

\displaystyle P_{i}^{n,3}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/4}}{k_{n}^{7/4}}\right),\ P_{i}^{n,4}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/2}}{k_{n}^{3/2}}\right),\ P_{i}^{n,5}=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}^{2}}\right),

thus
P_{i}^{n,3}+P_{i}^{n,4}+P_{i}^{n,5}\longrightarrow^{p}0
.
The above results together imply conclusion (
138
).

Directly applying the conclusion of Theorem
3
yields

\displaystyle\widehat{H_{n}^{1}}\longrightarrow^{p}\int_{0}^{T}\frac{(h_{1}(u,t,\sigma^{2}_{t}))^{2}}{\kappa^{4}}dt,

(139)

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=0}^{n-k_{n}}(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}h_{1}(u,t_{i+}^{n},\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}))\longrightarrow^{p}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{2}_{t}h_{1}(u,t,\sigma^{2}_{t}))dt.

(140)

Now, we prove

\displaystyle\widehat{H_{n}^{2}}\longrightarrow^{p}\int_{0}^{T}\frac{h_{1}(u,t,\sigma_{t}^{2})}{\kappa^{2}}h_{2}(t,\sigma_{t}^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2})dt.

(141)

We decompose

\displaystyle\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i+}^{n},\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\cdot\frac{1}{2k_{n}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n},\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\cdot\frac{1}{2k_{n}}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Bigg(\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i+}^{n},\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})-h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n},\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\frac{1}{2k_{n}}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Bigg(\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i+}^{n},\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\cdot\frac{1}{k_{n}}\Big(\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\ \cdot\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\quad\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i+}^{n},\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\cdot\frac{1}{2k_{n}}\left((\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle:=Q_{i}^{n,1}+Q_{i}^{n,2}+Q_{i}^{n,3}+Q_{i}^{n,4}.

For
Q_{i}^{n,1}
, we write

\displaystyle Q_{i}^{n,1}=

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\Bigg(\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\left(\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}}\left(\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}-\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\left(\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}}\left(\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}-\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)^{2}\right)\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\Bigg[\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\Bigg(\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}}\Big(\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\Big)^{2}\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}q_{i}^{n,1}(j)+\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\overline{q_{i}^{n,1}}(j).

Since

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}q_{i}^{n,1}(j)\right]=\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}\left[q_{i}^{n,1}(j)\right]\right]\equiv 0,

by applying Holder’s inequality with Lemma
4
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}q_{i}^{n,1}(j)\right)^{2}\right]=\frac{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}{9}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\sum_{|j_{2}-j_{1}|\leq k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[q_{i}^{n,1}(j_{1})q_{i}^{n,1}(j_{2})\right]\leq\frac{C}{k_{n}},

thus
\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}q_{i}^{n,1}(j)\longrightarrow^{p}0
.
And we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\overline{p_{i}^{n,1}}(j)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n},\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\left(a_{i}^{n}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}\Delta_{n}+3h_{2}\Delta_{n}\right)\right)\right]

(By using the results in Lemma
4
.)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\frac{h_{1}}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}+h_{2}\right)\right]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{p}\int_{0}^{T}\left(\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{\kappa^{4}}+\frac{h_{1}h_{2}}{\kappa^{2}}\right)dt.

Thus

\displaystyle Q_{i}^{n,1}\longrightarrow^{p}\int_{0}^{T}\left(\frac{h_{1}^{2}}{\kappa^{4}}+\frac{h_{1}h_{2}}{\kappa^{2}}\right)dt.

For
Q_{i}^{n,2}
, by using Holder’s inequality with Lemma
4
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}+1}\Big[\Big|\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i+}^{n},\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})-h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n},\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\frac{1}{2k_{n}}\left(\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)-\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)^{2}\Big|\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}+1}\Big[\left(\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i+}^{n},\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})-h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n},\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\right)^{2}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{k_{n}}}\left(\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}-\frac{\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}+1}\Big[\left(\frac{h_{1}(u,t_{i+}^{n},\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})-h_{1}(u,t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n},\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}\right)^{2}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{3}\left(\mathbb{E}_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}\left[\left(a_{i}^{n}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}\left(\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}}h_{1}\Delta_{n}+3h_{2}\Delta_{n}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=o_{p}(\Delta_{n}).

Furthermore, we can get
\mathbb{E}[|Q_{i}^{n,2}|]=o_{p}(1)
and
Q_{i}^{n,2}\longrightarrow^{p}0
. Similarly, we can prove that

\displaystyle Q_{i}^{n,3}=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\right),\ Q_{i}^{n,4}=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{k_{n}}\right).

thus
Q_{i}^{n,3}+Q_{i}^{n,4}\longrightarrow^{p}0
.
Combining the above results with (
139
) results in (
141
). Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\frac{\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}}{3}\left(\frac{3}{2k_{n}}\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}\right)^{2}-\frac{3}{k_{n}^{2}}h_{1}(u,\widehat{\sigma}_{t_{i+}^{n}}^{2})\right)\longrightarrow^{p}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma_{t}^{2}h_{2}(t,\sigma_{t}^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2})dt.

(142)

The conclusions follow from the results of (
138
)–(
142
).

The proof of Theorem
4
is finished.

\square

Proof of Theorem
5
:
For
\widehat{Var(V_{t}|\mathcal{F})}
, according to Mean Value Theorem, there exists some
\overline{\sigma_{t}^{2}}
between
\sigma_{t}^{2}
and
\widehat{\sigma}_{t}^{2}
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|\widehat{Var(V_{t}|\mathcal{F})}-Var(V_{t}|\mathcal{F})|]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[|h_{1}(u,t,\widehat{\sigma}_{t}^{2})-h_{1}(u,t,\sigma_{t}^{2})|]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[|h^{\prime}_{1}(u,t,\overline{\sigma_{t}^{2}})(\widehat{\sigma}_{t}^{2}-\sigma_{t}^{2})|]

(By Holder’s inequality.)

\displaystyle\leq(\mathbb{E}[|h^{\prime}_{1}(u,t,\overline{\sigma_{t}^{2}})|^{2}]\cdot\mathbb{E}[|(\widehat{\sigma}_{t}^{2}-\sigma_{t})|^{2}])^{1/2}

(Since
\sigma_{t}^{2}
and
\widehat{\sigma}_{t}^{2}
are bounded, together with Theorem 1 in
Liu et al. 2018
.)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

thus

\displaystyle\widehat{Var(V_{t}|\mathcal{F})}\longrightarrow^{p}Var(V_{t}|\mathcal{F}).

(143)

For
\widehat{Var(V^{\prime}_{t}|\mathcal{F})}
, from the proof of Theorem
2
, with
i=\lfloor t/\Delta_{n}\rfloor
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\widehat{Var(V^{\prime}_{t}|\mathcal{F})}-\int_{i\Delta_{n}}^{(i+k_{n})\Delta_{n}}h_{2}(t,\sigma_{t}^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2}))dt\longrightarrow^{p}0.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{1}{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\int_{i\Delta_{n}}^{(i+k_{n})\Delta_{n}}h_{2}(t,\sigma_{t}^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2}))dt-h_{2}(t,\sigma_{t}^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}_{t})^{2},(\tilde{\sigma}^{\prime}_{t})^{2})\right|\leq C\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}.

The above results together result in

\displaystyle\widehat{Var(V^{\prime}_{t}|\mathcal{F})}\longrightarrow^{p}Var(V^{\prime}_{t}|\mathcal{F}).

(144)

This finishes the proof.

\square

Proof of Corollary
1
:
According to Proposition 2.5 in
Podolskij and Vetter 2010
, the conclusions are direct results of Theorem
4
and Theorem
5
in Section
3.4
, together with Proposition
1
, Theorem
1
and Theorem
2
, respectively.

\square

8.5
Proofs for Section
4

Proof of Theorem
6
:
According to Theorem 2.7 in
Van der Vaart 2000
, the conclusion is a direct result of Theorem
1
, Theorem
2
and Theorem 1 in
Jacod and Todorov 2014
.
\square

Proof of Theorem
7
:
We note that the conclusion can be similarly proved by following the proof of Theorem
1
. We define, for
i=k_{n}+1,...,n-k_{n}
,

\displaystyle\tilde{s}_{i}^{n}

\displaystyle=\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\left(F(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})-F(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})\right)-\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\left(F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right).

By Mean Value Theorem, there exists some
\eta_{i+}^{n}
between
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}
and
(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))
,
\eta_{i-}^{n}
between
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}
and
(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))
, such that

\displaystyle\tilde{s}_{i}^{n}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot F^{\prime}(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot F^{\prime}(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\frac{F^{\prime\prime}(\eta_{i+}^{n})}{2}\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\frac{F^{\prime\prime}(\eta_{i-}^{n})}{2}\cdot\left(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}-(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))\right)^{2}

(Since
F
is twice continuously differentiable, and by using the boundedness of
\sigma
and

the condition (
9
),
b_{t,n}(u)=O_{p}(\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}})
, (
87
) and Lemma
1
.)

\displaystyle=F^{\prime}(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})\cdot s_{i}^{n}

\displaystyle\quad+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}\cdot\left(\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\vee\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\vee\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\right)\cdot(\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}+\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}})\right)\right),

where
s_{i}^{n}
is defined in the proof of Theorem
1
.
We decompose

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{L}}^{func}_{[0,T]}-\mathcal{L}^{func}_{[0,T]}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\tilde{s}_{i}^{n}+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\left(F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}}+b_{t_{i+}^{n},n}(u))-F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}}+b_{t_{i-}^{n},n}(u))\right)\right)-\mathcal{L}_{[0,T]}

\displaystyle(\text{Since $\gamma$ is bounded and with the condition \eqref{cond-gamma}.})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\tilde{s}_{i}^{n}+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X\cdot\left(F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})-F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})\right)\right)-\mathcal{L}^{func}_{[0,T]}+O_{p}(u^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1+\alpha-\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\tilde{s}_{i}^{n}+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\int_{t_{i-1}^{n}}^{t_{i}^{n}}\sigma_{s}dB_{s}\cdot\left(F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})-F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})\right)\right)-\mathcal{L}^{func}_{[0,T]}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\left(\Delta_{i}^{n}X-\int_{t_{i-1}^{n}}^{t_{i}^{n}}\sigma_{s}dB_{s}\right)\cdot\left(F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})-F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})\right)\right)+O_{p}(u^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1+\alpha-\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle(\text{By the proof of Lemma 2 in \cite[cite]{\@@bibref{Authors Phrase1YearPhrase2}{LLL2018}{\@@citephrase{(}}{\@@citephrase{)}}}, the boundedness of $\sigma$ and the condition \eqref{cond-vov}.})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}(F^{\prime}(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})\cdot s_{i}^{n})+\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}\left(\int_{t_{i-1}^{n}}^{t_{i}^{n}}\sigma_{s}dB_{s}\cdot\left(F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i+}^{n}})-F(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-}^{n}})\right)\right)-\mathcal{L}^{func}_{[0,T]}

\displaystyle\quad+O_{p}\left(u^{\beta-2}\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1+\alpha-\beta}{2}}+\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1}{\max\{\beta,r\}}-\frac{1}{2}}+\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1\wedge\frac{1}{(\max\{\beta,r\}+\epsilon)}}\cdot\left(\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\vee\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\vee\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\right)\cdot(\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}+\Delta_{n}^{1-\frac{\beta}{2}})\right)\right).

When
\alpha=1
and
\max\{\beta,r\}\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\cdot\left(\widehat{\mathcal{L}}^{func}_{[0,T]}-\mathcal{L}^{func}_{[0,T]}\right)=\sqrt{n}^{b\wedge(1-b)}\cdot\sum_{i=k_{n}+1}^{n-k_{n}}(F^{\prime}(\sigma^{2}_{t_{i-k_{n}-1}^{n}})\cdot s_{i}^{n})+o_{p}(1).

By following the proof of Theorem
1
with Lemma
2
, the conclusion can be obtained under the same conditions.

The proof of Theorem
7
is finished.
\square
